### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dimensional formula for magnetic flux is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mo>[</mo><mn>M</mn><msup> <mn>L</mn> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><msup> <mn>T</mn> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><msup> <mn>A</mn> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>]</mo> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math>  <mrow>   <mo>[</mo><msup>    <mn>M</mn>    <mn>0</mn>   </msup>   <msup>    <mn>L</mn>    <mrow>     <mo>&minus;</mo><mn>2</mn>    </mrow>   </msup>   <mtext>&thinsp;</mtext><msup>    <mn>T</mn>    <mn>2</mn>   </msup>   <msup>    <mn>A</mn>    <mrow>     <mo>&minus;</mo><mn>2</mn>    </mrow>   </msup>   <mo>]</mo>  </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math>  <mrow>   <mo>[</mo><mn>M</mn><msup>    <mn>L</mn>    <mn>3</mn>   </msup>   <mtext>&thinsp;</mtext><msup>    <mn>T</mn>    <mrow>     <mo>&minus;</mo><mn>2</mn>    </mrow>   </msup>   <mtext>&thinsp;</mtext><msup>    <mn>A</mn>    <mrow>     <mo>&minus;</mo><mn>2</mn>    </mrow>   </msup>   <mo>]</mo>  </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mo>[</mo><mn>M</mn><msup> <mn>L</mn> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><msup> <mn>T</mn> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><msup> <mn>A</mn> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>]</mo> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math> <mrow> <mo>[</mo><mn>M</mn><msup> <mn>L</mn> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><msup> <mn>T</mn> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><msup> <mn>A</mn> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>]</mo> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

According to Faraday&rsquo;s law of electromagnetic induction, E.M.F. =  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>d</mo><mi>&#981;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>d</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

so  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>&#981;</mn><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mn>E</mn><mn>M</mn><mn>F</mn><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mn>T</mn><mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mfrac><mn>W</mn><mi>q</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mo>[</mo><mn>T</mn><mo>]</mo></mrow></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>&#981;</mn><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mfrac><mrow><mn>M</mn><mn>L</mn><msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mn>T</mn><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>A</mn><mn>T</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mo>[</mo><mn>T</mn><mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><mn>M</mn><mn>L</mn><msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mn>T</mn><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mn>A</mn><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The velocity of water waves <math> <mi>v</mi> </math> may depend upon their wavelength <math> <mi>λ</mi> </math> , the density of water <math> <mi>ρ</mi> </math> and the acceleration due to gravity <math> <mi>g</mi> </math> . The method of dimensions gives the relation between these quantities as

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8733;</mo><mi>&#961;</mi><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>v</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>∝</mo><mi>g</mi><mi>λ</mi><mi>ρ</mi></mrow>  </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>v</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>∝</mo><mi>g</mi><mi>λ</mi></mrow>  </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>v</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>∝</mo><msup> <mi>g</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <msup> <mi>λ</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow>  </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>v</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>∝</mo><mi>g</mi><mi>λ</mi></mrow>  </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let  <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>v</mi> <mi>x</mi> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msup> <mi>g</mi> <mi>y</mi> </msup> <msup> <mi>λ</mi> <mi>z</mi> </msup> <msup> <mi>ρ</mi> <mi>δ</mi> </msup> <mo>.</mo></mrow>  </math> Now by substituting the dimensions of each quantities and equating the powers of <i>M</i>, <i>L</i> and <i>T</i> we get  <math> <mrow> <mi>δ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow>  </math> and  <math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </math> .

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> and <math> <mi>R</mi> </math> denote the inductance, capacitance and resistance respectively, the dimensional formula for <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>C</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>L</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow> </math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>ML</mi><mrow><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>L</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>L</mi></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p>As <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>L</mi><mi>R</mi></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mi>L</mi><mi>C</mi></msqrt></math> both have the dimensions of time, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>L</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Coefficient of thermal conductivity is the product of heat, distance and reciprocal of (area <math> <mo>×</mo> </math> difference in temperature <math><mo>×</mo></math> time). The new value of a unit of coefficient of thermal conductivity, if fundamental units are <math><mn>21.6</mn><mtext>kg</mtext></math>, <math> <mrow> <mtext>1 decimetre</mtext></mrow></math>, <math> <mrow> <mtext>4 K</mtext> </mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mtext>1 minute </mtext></mrow></math>will be ______ <math><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math> new units.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **02.50**

**Detailed Solution:**

Coefficient of thermal conductivity, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>Δθ</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mstyle><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>21.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>60</mn></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math> new units

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which two of the following five physical parameters have the same dimensions?
(A) Energy density
(B) Refractive index
(C) Dielectric constant
(D) Young’s modulus
(E) Magnetic field

**Options:**

- **(A)** (B) and (D)
- **(B)** (C) and (E)
- **(C)** (A) and (D)
- **(D)** (A) and (E)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) (A) and (D)**

**Detailed Solution:**

[Energy density]  <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>Work</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>d</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>e</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>Volume</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mtext> </mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>ML</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></math>

[Young’s modulus]   <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>F</mi> <mi>A</mi> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mi>l</mi> <mrow> <mi>Δ</mi><mi>l</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi>ML</mi><mtext>​ </mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mtext> </mtext><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>ML</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Two quantities <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math> are related by the relation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> is linear mass density and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is force. The dimensions of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> will be</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** Same as that of latent heat
- **(B)** Same a that of pressure
- **(C)** Same as that of work
- **(D)** Same as that of momentum

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Same as that of latent heat**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mfenced></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mi>force</mi><mrow><mi>mass</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>length</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mtext>B</mtext></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>force</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>length</mi></mrow><mi>mass</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mfrac><mi>energy</mi><mi>mass</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi>latent</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>heat</mi><mo>]</mo></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 259px; top: 130px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dependency of speed of water surface waves (capillary waves) on the density of water <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> their wavelength <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mo>)</mo></math> and surface tension <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is -

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Velocity of surface waves depends on density of water, its own wavelength of surface tension.

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0                 ..........(i)</mn></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1           .............(ii)</mn></math>


<math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1               .............(iii)</mn></math>

Solving
<math><mi mathvariant="normal"> a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2 </mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math><mi mathvariant="normal">, b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2 </mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math><mi mathvariant="normal">, c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2 ,</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>∝</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Find the dimensions of electric permittivity

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

From Coulomb&#39;s law, the force of attraction/repulsion between two point charges <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math> separated by distance <math><mi>r</mi></math> is

<math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#949;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#949;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>F</mi><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

Where <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#949;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> is electric permittivity.

Dimensions of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#949;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#949;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If speed <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> acceleration <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and force <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> are considered as fundamental units, the dimension of Young's modulus will be:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math><mi>Y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>V</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mi>Y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>K</mi><msup><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>K</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></math> Unit less

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>ML</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>LT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msup><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>MLT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msup><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>LT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></msup></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></msup></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

Dimensions of permeability (<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>) are

- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mi>L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>M</mi><mi>L</mi><msup><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>M</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><msup><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

<math> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><msub> <mi>I</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mi>I</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mi>l</mi></mrow> <mi>r</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> <math> <mo>⇒</mo> </math> <math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>F</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>A</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>M</mi><mi>L</mi><msup> <mi>T</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> <msup> <mi>A</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow> </math>

</details>

If in the measurement of the mean radius of the drop, the error is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>%,</mo></math> the error in the measurement of surface tension and density both are of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>%</mo></math>. Find the percentage error in measurement of the time period.

width="0.167em"></mspace><msup><mi>s</mi><mi>x</mi></msup><msup><mi>ρ</mi><mi>y</mi></msup><msup><mi>r</mi><mi>z</mi></msup></mrow></semantics></math> </p><p> <math><semantics><mrow><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>T</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>s</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><mi>x</mi></msup><msup><mrow><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>ρ</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><mi>y</mi></msup><msup><mrow><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>r</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><mi>z</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>M</mi><msup><mi>L</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><mi>y</mi></msup><msup><mrow><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>L</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><mi>z</mi></msup><mo accent="false">⇒</mo><mo form="prefix" stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>T</mi><mo form="postfix" stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>M</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi>L</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></semantics></math> </p><p> <math><semantics><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mspace width="0.167em"></mspace><mo accent="false">⇒</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">  </mtext><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">  </mtext><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></semantics></math> , <math><semantics><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></semantics></math> </p><p> <math><semantics><mrow><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>ρ</mi><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow><mi>s</mi></mfrac></msqrt><mo accent="false">⇒</mo><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="normal">ΔT</mtext><mi>T</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="normal">Δρ</mtext><mi>ρ</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="normal">Δr</mtext><mi>r</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="normal">Δs</mtext><mi>s</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></semantics></math> </p'>

</details>

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A uniform wire of length l and mass M is stretched between two fixed points, keeping a tension F. A sound of frequency <math> <mrow> <mi>&mu;</mi> </mrow> </math> is impressed on it. Then the maximum vibrational energy is existing in the wire when <math> <mrow> <mi>&mu;</mi> <mo>=</mo> </mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>ML</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>FL</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>FM</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>ML</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>ML</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

We should check it dimensionally

Let            <math> <mrow> <mi>&mu;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mo>K</mo> <msup> <mtext>F</mtext> <mtext>a</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>b</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math>

              <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>&mu;</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>F</mtext> <mtext>a</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>b</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

                  <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>MLT</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>b</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>b</mtext> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>c</mtext> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

     <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>b</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo> &rArr; </mo> <mtext>b</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> </math>

     <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>c</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo> &rArr; </mo> <mtext>c</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> </math>

     <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&rArr;   </mo> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

       <math> <mrow> <mtext>b</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

       <math d=""> <mrow> <mtext>c</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&rArr;   </mo> <mi>&mu;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mo>K</mo> <msup> <mtext>F</mtext> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </msup> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mi>&mu;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>K</mtext> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>ML</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

So only option (iv) satisfies it dimensionally.

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>v</mi></math> and <math><mi>a</mi></math> denote the displacement, the velocity and the acceleration of a particle executing simple harmonic motion of time period <math><mi>T</mi></math> , then which of the following do not change with time?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>a</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>v</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>a</mi><mi>T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> In option ,<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow><mi>v</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>s</mi></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>m</mi><mi>s</mi></mfrac></mfenced></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>0</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

So, according to the result above relation does not depend on time.

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>, force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math> and acceleration <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math> are chosen as the fundamental physical quantities, then the dimension of Young's modulus in terms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>Fa</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>Fa</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>Fa</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>KV</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></math>Unitless

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msup><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msup><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>ML</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>LT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msup><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>MLT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msup><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>LT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></msup><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></msup></mfenced><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>              <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>Fa</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The correct dimensions of the coefficient of viscosity <math><mi>η</mi></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mo>[</mo><mtext>M</mtext> <msup> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mo>-1</mo> </msup> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mo>[</mo><mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mo>[</mo><mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow><mo>]</mo> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mo>[</mo><mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mo>[</mo><mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow><mo>]</mo> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Viscous force is given as, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#960;&#951;</mi><mtext>r</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></math>.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#951;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mtext>r</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#951;</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mtext>r</mtext><mo>]</mo><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#951;</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mtext>MLT</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mtext>L</mtext><mo>]</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mtext>LT</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which of the following is the dimension, of Magnetic flux?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** $\mathrm{ML}^{2} \mathrm{T}^{-2} \mathrm{I}^{-1}$
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $\mathrm{ML}^{2} \mathrm{T}^{-2} \mathrm{I}^{-1}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∵</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Energy density in magnetic filed <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>α</mi><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>α</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo></math> Boltzmann constant
<math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo></math> temperature
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo></math> distance
The dimensions of <math><mi>β</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>K</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><msup><mi>MLT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>&#946;</mi></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The physical quantity which has the dimensional formula <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** Surface tension
- **(B)** Density
- **(C)** Solar constant
- **(D)** Compressibility

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) Solar constant**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>energy</mi><mrow><mi>area</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>time</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>= dimensions of the solar constant.

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If pressure <math> <mi>P</mi></math>, velocity <math> <mi>V</mi> </math> and time <math> <mi>T</mi> </math> are taken as fundamental physical quantities, the dimensional formula of force is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mi>P</mi><msup> <mi>V</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <msup> <mi>T</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow>  </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>P</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <msup> <mi>V</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <msup> <mi>T</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow>  </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mi>P</mi><mi>V</mi><msup> <mi>T</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow>  </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>P</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <mi>V</mi><msup> <mi>T</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow>  </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mi>P</mi><msup> <mi>V</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <msup> <mi>T</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow>  </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let  <math> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup> <mi>P</mi> <mi>x</mi> </msup> <msup> <mi>V</mi> <mi>y</mi> </msup> <msup> <mi>T</mi> <mi>z</mi> </msup> </mrow>  </math>  by substituting the following dimensions :  <math> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>P</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>M</mi><msup> <mi>L</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <msup> <mi>T</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow>  </math>  <math> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>V</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>L</mi><msup> <mi>T</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>T</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>T</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow>  </math>  and comparing the dimension of both sides  <math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow>  </math> , so  <math> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>P</mi><msup> <mi>V</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <msup> <mi>T</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow>  </math>

</details>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dependency of speed of water surface waves (capillary waves) on the density of water <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> their wavelength <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mo>)</mo></math> and surface tension <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is -

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Velocity of surface waves depends on density of water, its own wavelength of surface tension.

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0                 ..........(i)</mn></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1           .............(ii)</mn></math>


<math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1               .............(iii)</mn></math>

Solving
<math><mi mathvariant="normal"> a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2 </mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math><mi mathvariant="normal">, b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2 </mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math><mi mathvariant="normal">, c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2 ,</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>∝</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If force <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> length <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and time <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> be considered fundamental units, then the units of mass will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>[</mo><mi>F</mi><mi> </mi><mi>L</mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>[</mo><mi>F</mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>[</mo><mi>F</mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>L</mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>[</mo><mi>F</mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let  <math><mi>M</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> 

        Writing dimensions on both sides and using the principle of homogeneity of dimensions we have, 

         <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>L</mi><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup></math> 

        On comparing the power on both sides we get  <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  and  <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> 

        On solving we have  <math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  

          <math><mo>∴</mo></math>  units of mass is  <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>F</mi><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

---

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Force acting on a particle is given by <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> , where x is in metre and t is in seconds. The dimensions of B is -

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

F = ma,       we get

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>1</mn></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

The dimension of force = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>MLT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>
