### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span>The dimensional formula of gravitational constant is</span></p></div>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math> </span></p></div>
- **(B)** <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>ML</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math> </span></p></div>
- **(C)** <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math> </span></p></div>
- **(D)** <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span>None of these</span></p></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math> </span></p></div>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<div><p><span>From Newton&rsquo;s law of gravitation, we have: </span></p><p><span>       <math> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>G</mi><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>m</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mi>m</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> </span></p><p><span>or    <math> <mrow> <mi>G</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mi>m</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mi>m</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> </span></p><p><span>Substituting the dimensions of all physical quantities:</span></p><p><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>MLT</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow></mfrac></math> </span></p><p><span>or   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></math> </span></p><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span>Its SI unit is  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi>kg</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>or</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>kg</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo></math> </span></p></div>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo></math> energy, <math><mi>G</mi><mo>=</mo></math> gravitational constant, <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo></math> impulse and <math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo></math> mass, then dimensions of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>G</mi><mi>I</mi><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> are same as that of

**Options:**

- **(A)** Time
- **(B)** Mass
- **(C)** Length
- **(D)** Force

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Time**

**Detailed Solution:**

Dimensions of <math><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

Dimensions of <math><mi>G</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

Dimensions of <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

And dimension of <math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>G</mi><mi>I</mi><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math>

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If Surface tension<math><mi> </mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>)</mo></math>, Moment of Inertia <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>)</mo></math> and Planck's constant <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo></math> were to be taken as the fundamental units, the dimensional formula units, the dimensional formula for linear momentum would be:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>  
From comparing the power on both side 
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
From the above three equations we will get 
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mtext> </mtext><mo>&amp;</mo><mi> </mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dimensional formula for the capacitance of a capacitor is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>AM</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> <math><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>U</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>AT</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

---

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math> are physical quantities, having different dimensions, which of the following combinations can never be a meaningful quantity?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>PQ</mi><mo>−</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>PQ</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>PQ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>PR</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>PQ</mi><mo>−</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mfrac></math>

Since <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math> have different dimensions, therefore, their subtraction is not possible

</details>

---

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dimensions of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>, in the equation <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is pressure, <math><mi>x</mi></math> is distance and <math><mi>t</mi></math> is time, are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>L</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>P</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>a</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced></math>

Or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>b</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>P</mi></mfenced><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mi>L</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>L</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>M</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>T</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In a new system of units, the unit of force is <math> <mrow> <mtext>100 N</mtext></mrow></math>, unit of length is <math> <mrow> <mtext>10 m</mtext></mrow> </math> and unit of time is <math> <mrow> <mtext>100 s</mtext></mrow></math>. Unit of mass in this system can be expressed as

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mn>5</mn> </msup> <mtext> k</mtext><mtext>g</mtext></mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math  xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>   <mn>6</mn>  </msup>  <mtext> k</mtext><mtext>g</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math  xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mtext> k</mtext><mtext>g</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math  xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>   <mn>3</mn>  </msup>  <mtext> k</mtext><mtext>g</mtext></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mn>5</mn> </msup> <mtext> k</mtext><mtext>g</mtext></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Here, force (F) = [MLT<sup>–2</sup>] = 100 N             ...(i)

Length (L) = [L] = 10 m             ...(ii)

Time (t) = [T] = 100 s             ...(iii)

Substituting values of L and T from Equations. (ii) and (iii) in Equation. (i), we get

                   M × 10 × (100)<sup>–2</sup> = 100

or                   <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>M</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

or                   M = 100 × 1000 kg

                   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mtext>M</mtext><mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mn>5 </mn> </msup> <mtext>kg</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the time period <math> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>T</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> </math> of vibration of a liquid drop depends on surface tension <math> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>S</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> </math> , radius <math> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>r</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> </math> of the drop and density <math> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>ρ</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> </math> of the liquid, then the expression of <math> <mi>T</mi> </math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msqrt> <mrow> <mi>ρ</mi><msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>/</mo><mi>S</mi></mrow> </msqrt> </mrow>  </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msqrt> <mrow> <msup> <mi>ρ</mi> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> <msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>/</mo><mi>S</mi></mrow> </msqrt> </mrow>  </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msqrt> <mrow> <mi>ρ</mi><msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>/</mo><msup> <mi>S</mi> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </msqrt> </mrow>  </math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msqrt> <mrow> <mi>ρ</mi><msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>/</mo><mi>S</mi></mrow> </msqrt> </mrow>  </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let  <math> <mrow> <mi>T</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup> <mi>S</mi> <mi>x</mi> </msup> <msup> <mi>r</mi> <mi>y</mi> </msup> <msup> <mi>ρ</mi> <mi>z</mi> </msup> </mrow>  </math>  by substituting the dimension of  <math> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>T</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>T</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow>  </math>   <math> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>S</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>M</mi><msup> <mi>T</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>r</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>L</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>ρ</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>M</mi><msup> <mi>L</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow>  </math>  and by comparing the power of both the sides  <math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow>  </math>  so  <math> <mrow> <mi>T</mi><mo>∝</mo><msqrt> <mrow> <mi>ρ</mi><msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>/</mo><mi>S</mi></mrow> </msqrt> <mo>⇒</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msqrt> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>ρ</mi><msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mi>S</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> </msqrt> </mrow>  </math>

</details>

---

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If velocity, force and time are taken to be fundamental quantities, then the dimensional formula for mass is

**Options:**

- **(A)** Q = Kv<sup>-1</sup> FT
- **(B)** Q = Kv<sup>3</sup> FT<sup>2</sup>
- **(C)** Q = 2Kv<sup>-2</sup> FT
- **(D)** Q = -3Kv<sup>2</sup> FT

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Q = Kv<sup>-1</sup> FT**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let the quantity be Q, then,

Q = f (v, F, T)

Assuming that the function is the product of power functions of v, F and T,

Q = Kv<sup>x </sup>F<sup>y </sup>T<sup>z</sup>                                ...(i)

where K is a dimensionless constant of proportionality. The above equation dimensionally becomes

        [Q] = [LT<sup>-1</sup>]<sup>x</sup>[MLT<sup>-2</sup>]<sup>y</sup>[T]<sup>z</sup>

i.e.,   [Q] = [M<sup>y</sup>][L<sup>x</sup><sup>+</sup><sup>y</sup>T<sup>-x-2y</sup><sup>+z</sup>]            ...(ii)

​Now

Q = mass i.e.,   [Q] = [M]

So Equation (ii) becomes

[M] = [M<sup>y</sup>L<sup>x</sup><sup>+</sup><sup>y</sup>T<sup>-x-2y</sup><sup>+z</sup>]

its dimensional correctness requires

y = 1,     x + y = 0   and   - x - 2y + z = 0

which on solving yields

x = -1,    y = 1   and   z = 1

Substituting it in Equation (i), we get

Q = Kv<sup>-1</sup> FT

</details>

---

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In electromagnetic theory, the electric and magnetic phenomena are related to each other. Therefore, the dimensions of electric and magnetic quantities must also be related to each other. In the questions below, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>and</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced></math> stand for dimensions of electric and magnetic fields respectively, while <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">ϵ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>and</mi><mo> </mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> stand for dimensions of the permittivity and permeability of free space respectively. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mfenced><mo> </mo><mi>and</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mfenced></math> are dimensions of length and time respectively. All the quantities are given in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>SI</mi></math> units.
The relation between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>and</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

We have <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The potential energy of a particle varies with distance x from a fixed origin as <math><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> are constants. The dimensions of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> are -

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>       .....(i)</p><p>Where U = Potential energy</p><p><math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo></math> distance</p><p>Dimensional formula of potential energy, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Dimensional formula of distance, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>0</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>From equation (i), we can write</p><p><math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>]</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>0</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>0</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Dimensional formula of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>0</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>We can write equation (i) as,</p><p><math><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>U</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math ### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If gravitational constant <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> planks constant <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and speed of light <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> considered as fundamental physical quantities then find dimensional formula of time

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi> </mi><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi> </mi><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let 

 <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&sdot;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> 

 <math><mn>3</mn><mo>∝</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> 

 <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> 

On solving we get

 <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>γ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Dimensional analysis of the equation
<math><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>, 
gives the value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> as _______.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mtext>x</mtext></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mtext>P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#961;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mtext>P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#961;</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msup><mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mtext>M&#160;L</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mtext>T</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]"><msup><mtext>M&#160;L</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced></mfrac><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
But <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> is the dimension of velocity.
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Suppose a quantity can be dimensionally represented in terms <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>, that is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>. The quantity mass

**Options:**

- **(A)** maybe represented in terms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math> if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** maybe represented in terms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math> if <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>≠</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** Can always be dimensionally represented in terms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>
- **(D)** Can never be dimensionally represented in terms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) maybe represented in terms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math> if <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>≠</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>M=</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>.</mo><msup><mi>L</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mfrac><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math> should not be zero.

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The frequency of vibration <math><mi>f</mi></math> of a mass m suspended from a spring of spring constant k is given by a relation of the type <math><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></math> where c is a dimensionless constant. The values of <math><mi>x</mi></math> and <math><mi>y</mi></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo></math>

Spring constant <math><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>.</mo></mrow></mrow></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msup><msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mi>MT</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi></math> or <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

Therefore, <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If unit of length, mass and time each be doubled, the new unit of work done is _____ times the old unit of work

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Given, <math><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

---

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The viscosity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>η</mi></math> of a gas depends on the long-range attractive part of the intermolecular force, which varies with molecular separation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> according to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mi> </mi><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mi> </mi><mi>μ</mi><msup><mi>r</mi><mrow><mo mathvariant="italic">–</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi></mrow></msup></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> is a number and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>μ</mi></math> is a constant. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>η</mi></math> is a function of the mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> of the molecules, their mean speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi></math>, and the constant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>μ</mi></math> then which of following is correct -

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#951;</mi><mo>&#8733;</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>&#956;</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>η</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">+</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">-</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi>μ</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#951;</mi><mo>&#8733;</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></msup><msup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi>&#956;</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#951;</mi><mo>&#8733;</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>v</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mi>&#956;</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>η</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">+</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">-</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi>μ</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≡</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>≡</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≡</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

Dimensions of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">‘</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">’</mi></math> depend on mass <math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> mean speed <math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></math> and constant <math><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></math> as -

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

Equating dimensions both sides

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>

<math><mn>a</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In the formula, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math> and <math><mi>c</mi></math> have dimensions of electric capacitance and magnetic field intensity respectively. The dimensions of <math><mi>b</mi></math> in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>.</mo></math> system are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math> <mo>⇒   </mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

Writing dimensions for <math><mi>a</mi></math> and <math><mi>c</mi></math>, we have

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>b</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Q</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>V</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mfenced><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mo>]</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><msup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math>

    <math><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

</details>
