### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dimensions of resistance are the same as (where <math><mi>h</mi></math> is the Plank's constant and <math><mi>e</mi></math> is the charge)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Resistance, <math><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>W</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>q</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Position of a body with acceleration <math><mi>a</mi></math> is given by <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo></math> Here <math><mi>t</mi></math> is time. Find the dimensions of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math>.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi> </mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi> </mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Positions <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

On comparing both sides

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

 <math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dimensional formula for areal velocity is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>]</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Areal velocity is the area covered per unit time.

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>r</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>r</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>v</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>t</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> where <math><mi>v</mi></math> is velocity, <math><mi>t</mi></math> is time and <math><mi>A</mi><mo>, </mo><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> are constants, then the dimensional formula of <math><mi>B</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Dimension of velocity = <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mn>M</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mn>L</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mn>T</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

<math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mi>v</mi> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mi>B</mi><msup> <mi>t</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mi>B</mi> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mi>v</mi> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <msup> <mi>t</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The quantity <math> <mrow> <mi>X</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>ε</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi>L</mi><mi>V</mi></mrow> <mi>t</mi> </mfrac></mrow></math>where <math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>ε</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math> is the permittivity of free space, <math> <mi>L</mi> </math> is length, <math> <mi>V</mi> </math> is the potential difference and <math><mi>t</mi> </math> is time. The dimensions of <math> <mi>X</mi> </math> are the same as that of

**Options:**

- **(A)** Resistance
- **(B)** Charge
- **(C)** Voltage
- **(D)** Current

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) Current**

**Detailed Solution:**

[  <math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>ε</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi>L</mi></mrow>  </math> ] = [C] 
∴ <math> <mrow> <mi>X</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>ε</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi>L</mi><mi>V</mi></mrow> <mi>t</mi> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mi>C</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>V</mi></mrow> <mi>t</mi> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mi>Q</mi> <mi>t</mi> </mfrac> </mrow>  </math> = current

</details>

---

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>E</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> denote electric and magnetic fields respectively, which of the following is dimensionless?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfrac><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msqrt><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi></math> (Velocity of light if we consider free space) and <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

So <math><msqrt><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is dimensionless.

</details>

---

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>; then

**Options:**

- **(A)** the dimensions of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> are the same
- **(B)** the dimensions of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> are the same
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> is dimensionless quantity
- **(D)** none of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> is dimensionless quantity**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi><mtext>sin</mtext><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><mtext>cos</mtext><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>; Dimensions of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> would be same as two quantities can be added only when they have same dimensions. So from given equation, we can say that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> is dimensionless.

</details>

---

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Dimensional formula of magnetic flux is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#966;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>F</mi><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>MLT</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>ML</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

</details>

---

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If velocity, force and time are taken as the fundamental quantities, then using dimensional analysis choose the correct dimensional formula for mass among the following. <math> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo> </math> <math> <mi>K</mi> </math> is a dimensionless constant<math> <mo stretchy="false">]</mo> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>K</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo> </mo><mi>F</mi><mo> </mo><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>K</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo> </mo><mi>F</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi>K</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo> </mo><mi>F</mi><mo> </mo><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo> </mo><mi>K</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo> </mo><mi>F</mi><mo> </mo><mi>T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>K</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo> </mo><mi>F</mi><mo> </mo><mi>T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let the quantity be Q, then,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

Assuming that the function is the product of power functions of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>F</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math>,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><mi>K</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">x</mi></msup><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><msup><mi>F</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">y</mi></msup><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><msup><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">z</mi></msup><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><mo mathvariant="italic">.</mo><mo mathvariant="italic">.</mo><mo mathvariant="italic">.</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>)</mo></math>     

where K is a dimensionless constant of proportionality. The above equation dimensionally becomes

    <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo>[</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>]</mo><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mo>[</mo><mi>L</mi><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mo>]</mo><mi>x</mi></msup><mo>[</mo><mi>M</mi><mi>L</mi><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mo>]</mo><mi>y</mi></msup><mo>[</mo><mi>T</mi><msup><mo>]</mo><mi>z</mi></msup></math>

i.e.,   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>]</mo><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>M</mi><mi>y</mi></msup><msup><mi>L</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>, ​Now

Q = mass i.e.,   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><mi>M</mi><mo>]</mo></math>

So Equation (ii) becomes

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi>M</mi><mo>]</mo><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>M</mi><mi>y</mi></msup><msup><mi>L</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
its dimensional correctness requires 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo> </mo></math>and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo> </mo><mo>-</mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo> </mo><mo>+</mo><mo> </mo><mi>z</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>0</mn></math>

which on solving yields

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo></math>and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

Substituting it in Equation (i), we get

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mi>K</mi><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mo mathvariant="italic">-</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">1</mn></mrow></msup><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><mi>F</mi><mo mathvariant="italic"> </mo><mi>T</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The viscosity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>η</mi></math> of a gas depends on the long-range attractive part of the intermolecular force, which varies with molecular separation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> according to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mi> </mi><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mi> </mi><mi>μ</mi><msup><mi>r</mi><mrow><mo mathvariant="italic">–</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi></mrow></msup></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> is a number and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>μ</mi></math> is a constant. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>η</mi></math> is a function of the mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> of the molecules, their mean speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi></math>, and the constant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>μ</mi></math> then which of following is correct -

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#951;</mi><mo>&#8733;</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>&#956;</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>η</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">+</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">-</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi>μ</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#951;</mi><mo>&#8733;</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></msup><msup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi>&#956;</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#951;</mi><mo>&#8733;</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>v</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mi>&#956;</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>η</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">+</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">n</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">-</mo><mn mathvariant="italic">1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup><mo> </mo><msup><mi>μ</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≡</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>≡</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≡</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

Dimensions of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">‘</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">’</mi></math> depend on mass <math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> mean speed <math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></math> and constant <math><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></math> as -

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

Equating dimensions both sides

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>

<math><mn>a</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>p</mi></math> represents radiation pressure, <math><mi>C</mi></math> represents the speed of light and <math><mi>q</mi></math> represents radiation energy striking a unit area per second, then non-zero integers <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></math> and <math><mi>c</mi></math> are such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>p</mi><mi>a</mi></msup><msup><mi>q</mi><mi>b</mi></msup><msup><mi>C</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></math> is dimensionless, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Here, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

Or       <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup></math>]

Comparing powers of <math><mi>M</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>L</mi></math> and <math><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo></math> we get

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

Solving <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> (velocity of light), <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi></math> (Planck's constant) and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>G</mi></math> (Universal gravitational constant) are taken as fundamental quantities, then the dimensional formula of mass is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi>M</mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup></math> ......(i)

Where, <math><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>L</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mi>G</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><mi>L</mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

Comparing the coefficients M, L, T, of both sides we get

<math><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> ......(ii)

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> ......(iii)

<math><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> .....(iv)

Solve the equations (ii), (iii) and (iv), we get

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

So, <math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If force <math><mi>F</mi></math> is related with distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math> and time <math><mi>t</mi></math> as <math><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> , the dimensions of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

All terms of an equation must have same dimensions

 <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> 



 <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> 

 <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A spherical body, of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> and radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math>, is allowed to fall in a medium of viscosity <math><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi></math> . The time in which the velocity of the body increases from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>63</mn></math> times the terminal velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is called time constant <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">τ</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> . Dimensionally <math><mi mathvariant="normal">τ</mi></math> can be represented as

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>πη</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi></mrow><msup><mi>g</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mi>m</mi><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi><mi>r</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) None of these**

**Detailed Solution:**

Time constant is the time of free fall of a body under gravity in the viscous medium, during which the velocity of the body increases to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>63</mn><mo>%</mo></math> of the terminal velocity. Dimensionally, none of the alternatives have dimensions of time.

</details>

---

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If electronic charge <math><mi>e</mi></math>, electron mass <math><mi>m</mi></math>, speed of light in vacuum <math><mi>c</mi></math> and Planck's constant <math><mi>h</mi></math> are taken as fundamental quantities, the permeability of vacuum <math><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> can be expressed in units of:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Magnetic potential energy inside a solenoid is

<math><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>L</mi><msup><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> Where L = self-inductor

<math><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>A</mi><mi> </mi><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mo>∵</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo></math> no. of turns per unit length

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>A</mi><mi>l</mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi>A</mi><mi>l</mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>U</mi><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

Dimensionally, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>′</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi>l</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q60. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the units of mass, length and time are doubled then unit of angular momentum will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** Doubled
- **(B)** Tripled
- **(C)** Quadrupled
- **(D)** Eight times the original value

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) Quadrupled**

**Detailed Solution:**

The dimensions of angular momentum are 

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>J</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

When units of mass, length and time are doubled, than

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>J</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>&prime;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>J</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>&prime;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

Unit of angular momentum is quadrupled.

</details>

---

### Q61. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Time <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, velocity <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and angular momentum <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> are chosen as fundamental quantities instead of mass, length and time. In terms of these, the dimensions of mass would be:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>]</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mi>TC</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>]</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>M</mi><mo>&#8733;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>1</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>]</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=[</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></msup></math>]

<math><mo>&#10233;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi> </mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="italic"> x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">solving</mi></math>
<math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="italic">and x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mn>1</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

---

### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What is the dimensional formula of co-efficient of viscosity?

**Options:**

- **(A)** Dimensional formula: <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** Dimensional formula: <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** Dimensional formula: <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** Dimensional formula: <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Dimensional formula: <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Coefficient of viscosity <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

F = tangential Force,

A = Area, x = distance between the layers,

v = velocity.

Dimensional Formula of Force <math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Dimensional Formula of Area <math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Dimensional Formula of distance <math><msup><mrow><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Dimensional Formula of velocity <math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Putting these values in above equation we get,

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">η</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q63. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dimensional formula for entropy is

**Options:**

- **(A)** [<math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>]
- **(B)** [<math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>]
- **(C)** [<math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>]
- **(D)** [<math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) [<math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>]**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>E</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>t</mi><mi>r</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>p</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>h</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>p</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>r</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>t</mi><mi>u</mi><mi>r</mi><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi></math> <math> <mrow>  <mrow><mo>(</mo>   <mrow>    <mtext>&#x0394;</mtext><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>T</mi><mtext>&#x0394;</mtext><mi>S</mi></mrow>  <mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></math>

[Entropy]=<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>

</details>

### Q64. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> denote the dimensional formula of the permittivity of vacuum. If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>=</mo></math> mass, <math><mtext>L</mtext><mo>=</mo></math> length, <math><mtext>T</mtext><mo>=</mo></math> time and <math><mtext>A</mtext><mo>=</mo></math> electric current, then dimensions of permittivity is given as <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>L</mtext> <mtext>q</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>r</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mtext>s</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>. Find the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mtext>p</mtext><mo>-</mo><mtext>q</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>r</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

From Coulomb's law,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ε</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>
